The prepared native loader consumes the complete standalone namespace and independently authenticates all main tensor files through the existing bounded parallel file-owner implementation. Exact generated config/index/card pins agree with the previously captured complete export plan. The preserved original parent configuration separately owns tokenizer, vision and draft metadata. The public checkpoint parser still refuses unqualified quantization layouts. Existing overlay defaults remain unchanged; standalone arithmetic additionally binds the complete export digest into prefix identity.

Metadata inspection is separated from model admission. The compiled standalone research allowlist is empty until a complete export and independent byte audit provide its exact manifest digest. An arbitrary caller-supplied digest or completion flag cannot enable execution. The supported registry remains unchanged. The bounded conversation and existing Engine arithmetic/residency/prefix/cancellation/HTTP diagnostic can later consume an admitted standalone export; their physical limits are unchanged. A future standalone Engine check keeps the original tokenizer as an independent oracle while the loaded engine uses the exported directory.

The new weights-free native checks cover the exact file namespace and complete byte ledger, changed component pins, incomplete coverage, optional-draft metadata, malformed/path/extent inputs, trust-anchor mismatch, source mutation, symlink refusal, metadata limits, unchanged parent component selection, cache separation and refusal of self-authorized model admission. These are added checks, not executed results: local builds remain deferred while the single heavy held-out campaign runs. The new code's compilation, catalogue and Mac acceptance are pending CI. Actual full export, native payload loading, arithmetic parity and product conversations remain pending.

Earlier standalone storage/preparation commit 3e2f314d38841dfcfa7eba0fb6c1a14c7bde836f passes complete engine CI 37222474301, including all three jobs. Image-workspace commit cc4f23bca5169cb387813533af2989d480911c8c passes complete Mac CI 37223102881 and its engine coverage/public-library jobs. Its engine workflow 37223102967 fails the brain gate because the evidence record lacked the required machines field. The exact failure is preserved here; the field was repaired in d8b8d975e58ddf03ebb552fd87d4ea3b93cdc2b9. Do not describe that failed full engine workflow as passing. The subsequent product-driver commit reruns the full fixed-store engine gates.

Raw source and complete CI evidence follow. Home paths are normalized to <HOME>. Original and normalized byte counts and SHA-256 digests are retained; compression is checked by roundtrip reconstruction. No model payloads, logits or partial held-out answers are embedded. No speed, quality, supported-pack or installed-artifact claim follows.
